## Prerequisites and recording rules

M = one modern iPhone simulator; S = one smaller iPhone simulator. Before execution record actual model, UDID, iOS version, build SHA and role for each. Both roles = separate authorized client and caregiver QA accounts; never record their passwords/tokens. The live API is `https://new.carezaar.com/api/`; verify current contract before mutating QA data. Existing accounts and ignored credential files are preferred over new accounts. Any controlled URLProtocol/launch fixture must be labelled; it does not prove a live backend outcome.

Use native Simulator/Appium/XCTest interactions, actual visible UI and redacted network/state evidence. Do not use web behavior as Android or iOS proof. Do not upload real identity data or live personal camera/media. Physical-only tests remain NOT RUN until attempted, then BLOCKED when unavailable, not N/A. Authorized account deletion needs safely expendable QA data; do not delete existing client QA accounts merely to tick a case. Track original failure, fix, retest and related regression. Retry genuine blockers at phase 25 before assigning final outcomes.

Evidence paths should identify exact screenshots/logs/xcresults and observations, including request counts, payload field names, timer intervals, delayed navigation and persistence when relevant. Check free disk before builds/boots and preserve source, credentials and QA evidence. Record any phase-order departure caused by dependencies.

## Group A–I trace audit

- A startup/reference/session: phases 1, 2, 8, 9, 26; all §4 branches and request/cache evidence.
- B auth/signup/password: phases 2, 3, 6, 22, 25, 27; actual auth UI and exact symbol cases.
- C preferences/location/payload: phases 3, 6, 8, 11, 25; zero/one/multiple certifications and honest unavailable location.
- D matching/profile/success: phases 4, 5, 16, 17, 19, 23; both-role relationship matrix, action state/errors and once-only loading.
- E management/introduction/history: phases 4, 15, 23, 25; pending label, first line/full modal/no reviews.
- F messages: phases 4, 9, 10, 16, 19, 27; sequential/burst sends, unread, timestamps and scroll position.
- G notifications: phases 5, 10, 11, 14, 24, 25; modal/Show/fallback/poll lifecycle.
- H settings/network/pagination: phases 4, 7, 8, 15, 17, 25, 27; FAQ text, password/session, refresh metadata, retry/cancel and empty-page policy.
- I lifecycle/accessibility/layout: phases 4–6, 10–12, 17–18, 20–21, 26, 28; actual small/modern native interactions and evidence.

## Bug, blocker and release decision records

For each failure: ID, severity P0/P1/P2/P3, device/OS/build, role, screen, preconditions, numbered exact steps, expected, actual, reproducibility, evidence, likely owner (iOS/backend/environment), fix attempted and retest result. Keep the original FAIL evidence when later passing after fix.

For each blocker: case ID, precise missing dependency, attempts/logs/local safe fixes, retry outcome and required action. Continue independent cases. At final handoff there must be no undocumented NOT RUN rows. Summarize PASS/PASS-after-fix/FAIL/BLOCKED/N/A counts without double-counting parameterized states or unit tests as separate manual passes.

### October 12: corrupted persisted reference-cache recovery

Online corrupted-cache recovery now closes P01-006. Malformed JSON and a parseable cache missing one FAQ passed on Modern in 39.836 and 41.966 seconds, and on Small in 83.964 and 45.892 seconds. Each run retained the actual corrupt file through installation and native loading, made exactly 23 reference GETs, repaired all 22 datasets and opened Sign In through the normal button. Root and independent review verified 28 artifacts and the repaired caches. These were shared anonymous startup checks; local TokenStore clearance was QA setup, with no backend account, profile, message, relationship or media writes. Offline recovery, incomplete server responses and ordinary-relaunch metadata completion remain separate checks. All 57 production source hashes still match the preserved 107-unit snapshot; the added UI method supplies regression coverage without changing the app. Public proof: `oct12-invalid-cache-recovery-anonymous-proof`. Each repaired cache has schema 3, the exact 22 dataset keys and 21 server-advertised count keys, unique IDs, and a marker/count match against a separate fresh public metadata read. The five-second delay affects timing only; backend responses are real. Original valid caches, corrupt bytes, native logs/xcresults and the first non-witnessed Modern run remain private and preserved. Small Splash clipping remains within open P17 scope. The task-owned Small simulator was removed only after its six DCIM files and cache/results were preserved; pre-existing Modern was shut down and preserved.
